tbmm-2020-06-24sit05spe0205par0329	Mr. President, I have made a pledge on my own behalf on the first chapter of the Law Proposal on the Law Act and Changes in Some Laws. I salute our Veteran Assembly. One of the main goals of the Judicial Reform Strategy Document, announced by our President, was the simplification of the legal trial and boosting its effectiveness. The arrangement in this framework is the continuation of reforms that have been implemented so far, in an effort to increase the efficiency of justice in the judiciary and to complete the trials in a reasonable amount of time. The preparation process of the proposal has a long history and has emerged as a result of an extremely thorough study. The proposal was drafted by the Supreme Court, Prosecutor's Office, all ministries, relevant institutions and organisations, the Association of Bars of Turkey, universities with law schools, law associations, and the opinions of 100 different institutions and organisations. Our law proposal is a reformary provision designed to determine and resolve the ineffective aspects of the Law of Law, to review the preliminary study institution, to make judicial procedures more functional, to effectively use alternative conflict solutions in the judicial judiciary, and to exclude the elements that prevent it. In this framework, it aims to review the Laws of Laws within the framework of principles that govern the judicial judiciary, to be carried out more effectively and efficiently, to resolve the judicial problems expressed by the practitioners and academics. Mr. President, in light of some innovations in our proposal, in the current application, all you're going to receive is being sued, but it is impossible for the plaintiff to determine the exact amount and value of your case when the trial is launched, or to make that determination is impossible for the plaintiff. In order for this problem to be a solution, it is envisioned by the court to determine the exact moment when it is possible to determine the amount and value you will receive. Again, the application of the evidence advance to be submitted in advance to the court's clerk's desk will be lifted with an obligation to deposit this advance on the court's desk in advance, due to a heavy financial burden to the plaintiffs and the result of a violation of the freedom of rights search. In order to prevent unnecessary lengthening of the cases and to resolve the conflict, the court's decision to dismiss the judge's rejection can be applied to the law immediately, depending on the amount and value of the case. Again, with the regulation, it is possible to be reformed until the investigation is over, if a process is carried out by the Supreme Court or by the district court is carried out, so that the workload can be reduced and the principle of the procedure economy can be implemented effectively. Mr. President, valued lawmakers, as a result, will ensure that more than 4.2 million cases, including a first-degree trial, and a review of the law, will result in a much faster and more effective outcome. Again, this proposal will directly affect the sides of more than 4 million legal cases, the lawyers or judges, the judges, the judges, the referees. Thus, the proposal aims to address the problems that prolong the trial and to boost the confidence in the judiciary. I salute our Veteran Assembly, wishing our proposal to our country, to our legal system, to our judicial community.

tbmm-2020-06-24sit05spe0208par0353	Mr. President, I have made a commitment to the motion on Article 1 of the proposed law. I salute the General Assembly. Obviously, this clause prevents parties from losing their rights, but when we look at the overall proposal, we've found that there are some setbacks. More and more fundamental laws have been changed in recent years. Almost all of these changes were brought to the House in a hurry, without discussion, without the opinion of the relevant individuals and institutions, and soon under law. The hurrying of the law soon revealed the obligation to change the same law over and over again, causing it to work in a vicious loop, causing it to function. The Law on Laws Number 1086 has changed 33 times in eighty-four years from 1927 to 2011, while the law you passed in 2011 has changed 19 times. In addition to disrupting the system and spirit of the law, this change led to the implementation of the relevant individuals and institutions without any discussion. There is a saying that good laws cause better things to be done, and worse things to be done. Members of parliament, most of the changes made so far have been made under the name of reform, with the aim of accelerating the trial, but the trial has become more severe. This proposal, drawn up in the Court of Law 6100, has come before us to be discussed in the General Assembly, considering the demands from the judicial community, the Constitutional Court's decisions, the Court of Appeals and the implementation of the district court, in order to speed up the trial in Law Code 6100, to make the right to a fair trial more effective. When reviewing these proposal clauses, it is understood that some provisions in Articles 3, 20, 102 and 398 will be annulled by the Constitutional Court and that new regulations will be made in this issue. From October 1, 2011, the Law of Laws No. 6100 was criticised by some of the provisions of the law, especially the enforcers and the doctrine. In this regard, court decisions have been assessed in the Supreme Court hearings in various ways by academics and lawyers at the university and their criticism of the law and proposals to enforce it. With the 20th article of this law proposal, Article 6100, Article 183/A is added to Article 183/A, and the method of the work of the courts, such as the original trade court, has been established in front of the committee. The regulation will enable the appointment of the regent judge to speed up the trial in court. In addition, the provision provided is the equivalent of Article 5 of Law 5235. The 21st amendment to the proposal of the law provides an unprovoked ban on the 186th amendment of the HMK, which regulates the method of verbal trial. Although verbal trial is considered the most important part of the legal trial, the HMC has been seen to have been used as a means of prolonging the trial after the examination has ended. With this arrangement, this abuse will be prevented by the completion of the oral trial within two weeks. The 22nd article of the proposal of the law has been subject to bilateral discrimination as individuals, illiterates, and illiterates who cannot sign the 206th article of the HMC. With the regulation, it is mandatory that notary notars notarized the signing of those who can read and write, and those who can't read or write and who can't write and who can't sign the bond in a valid manner. In this way, the two situations are divided according to the balance of the protected interest. The 23rd article of the proposal of the law is made an important determination on the date when the terms of the law shall be judged by amendments to Article 215. The doctrine and the implementation of the judiciary support the positive arrangement. Under the regulation, it is legal to enforce the date of the fixed terms of the doctrine and judicial decisions that will be valid. Under the 24th article of the proposal of the law, under an important principle of the law, no one can use or endure the document he has set up as evidence in his own behalf. Despite this principle, the statement of Article 222 of the HMK came to the conclusion that if the trial did not contain any records from the other side's business books, it could provide evidence in favor of the entity based on its own business books. This arrangement was criticised by almost all lawyers. With this regulation in Article 222 of the HMK, the way to which the side of the trial can endure a binding piece of evidence is closed. The 25th amendment to the HMK 281 provision of the proposed law has been enabled to extend expert reports, deadlines for counterresponsibility and objections. Although there may have been a request for an extension against the petition, there has been a contradiction in the trial that there is no request for extensions against expert reports, which may be more comprehensive than lawsuits and response petitions, this conflict has been resolved. Despite the need for the judge to write all operations during the discovery of the HMK 290, it was found that judges did not write their observations on the discovery site and the site of the law proposal, which was laid out as a necessity for the judge to write observations on the subject of discovery and the site. The 27, 28 and 29th article of the proposal of the law has long been removed by adding 305/A to the HMK with the change package. With the current arrangement, the lack of the judge's judgment about one of the parties' demands or a few of them -- such as judicial costs -- should not be judged, even if there are no demands -- would not be resolved through compensation and concessions. In other words, in the event of such a defect, the only way to repair this deficiency was to file a complaint or appeal. With this arrangement, the obligation for judicial decisions to be charged and appealed for this reason alone has been eliminated. Thus, the decisions will not be taken into account and appeal only for this reason, but will be added to the sentence of the first-degree court of decrees that have been forgotten. It was a controversial issue in which to make a decision on the 30th and 31st amendment of the proposal, in which the waiver, acceptance and peace proceedings could be carried out before the verdict was finalized, but in the case of the first-degree court of judgment, in the case of a waivement, acceptance and peace proceedings, the decision on waivement, acceptance and peace was made by whom would be adopted by the first-level court, after which the provisions of Article 310 and 314 have been carried out. The debate on the 32th article of the proposal of the law and the extension of the period in the application of the HMK 317 (as part of the Article 317) as its application, and on the application of this motion, the period will be extended by the court. With Article 33 of the proposed law, the application fee paid at the opening of the case was essentially a fee for trial, but the HMK 323 provision was not accepted as a charge of trial, and it was a positive development that the application fee was accepted as a cost of trial by the amendment in Article 323. With Article 35 of the law proposal, in the current regulation, in the absence of a restraining order, although it was possible to refer to the decision on the objection, there was no right to take credit for the decision against the decision made by the party, which was made in HMK 3441 has been eliminated whether the decisions were made face-to-face or absence of the entity. In what circumstances could the district court courts, which have contributed positively to the dissolution of the law since it entered into effect, have sparked a variety of debates in the doctrine, in which it could be held without trial, in which case the law proposal could be settled, in which case important evidence may be collected that might be effective in the resolution of the HMK 353 decree. Under Article 39 of the proposed law, the regulation of the HMK 359 shall be removed from the obligation to make a valid decision on matters that are not necessary for legal security, just to write a reason. Work and time will be saved for a change.
